# Card catalog — 99 traits, two faces each

> Generated by `npm run catalog` from `src/data/cards.js`. Do not edit by hand.

Every trait is a card with two faces. The **Physical** face costs AP, has a short cooldown and scales with **P** (`base + P`). The **Ethereal** face costs AP plus an etherion of the card’s colour, has a longer cooldown, and does what the physical face cannot — a *capability* that scales with **E** (P may also scale a mundane sub-part). Playing either face sends the whole card to the track for that face’s cooldown (+ duration).

An Ethereal face is magic that must be awakened: it cannot be played until its card has E 1 (`T.RULES.ETHEREAL_MIN_E`). Every hero sets out with at least two cards awakened (`START_AWAKE`). Until 2.3 a face could be played at E 0.

Each line shows the face at **P0 / E1** and, in italics, at **P3 / E3**. ★ = signature trait, ☆ = minor signature. Caps: P 3, E 3. A hero starts with 6 cards and may grow to 9 — never more than 3 of 9 from one bloodline.

## 0 · Human — leans T/C

*Leverage, not weaponry. Reads minds, builds tools, outlasts, coordinates.*

**Theory of Mind** ★ · Time · `hum_mind`

- *Phys — Read* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Mark a foe: the next hit on it deals +2. *(P3: Mark a foe: the next hit on it deals +2, and its own next hit deals −1.)*
- *Eth — Pre-empt* · 0 AP + 2 Time · CD 3 — Read a foe: this round its attack is Aimed, at the space its target stands on now. Step away and it strikes whoever stands there then, foe or pilot. *(P3/E3: Read a foe and every foe within 2 of it: this round its attack is Aimed, at the space its target stands on now. Step away and it strikes whoever stands there then, foe or pilot.)*

**Tool Use** ★ · Space · `hum_tool`

- *Phys — Improvise* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Your next strike this turn deals +1. *(P3: Your next strike this turn deals +4.)*
- *Eth — Reach Rig* · 1 AP + 1 Space · CD 2 — Interact with an objective up to 2 nodes away (not one up on a ledge), and brace behind the rig: Shield: the next hit on you is 1 less. *(P3/E3: Interact with an objective up to 4 nodes away (not one up on a ledge), and brace behind the rig: Shield: the next hit on you is 3 less.)*

**Persistence** · Time · `hum_persist`

- *Phys — Pace* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Shrug off Anchored/Weakened, then move 1. *(P3: Shrug off Anchored/Weakened, then move 4.)*
- *Eth — Second Wind* · 0 AP + 2 Time · CD 3 — Brace to recover: at round end, regain all the vitality you lose from now until then, up to 6. *(P3/E3: Brace to recover: at round end, regain all the vitality you lose from now until then, up to 10.)*

**Throwing Arm** · Mass · `hum_throw`

- *Phys — Hurl* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Zone strike for 2. *(P3: Zone strike for 5.)*
- *Eth — Heavy Throw* · 0 AP + 2 Mass · CD 3 — Throw your ally from beside you onto a free space within 3 of you, ledges included. *(P3/E3: Throw your ally from beside you onto a free space within 5 of you, ledges included.)*

**Language** ☆ · Charge · `hum_lang`

- *Phys — Call* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Call an ally: Guide them 1, to a space you choose, now. *(P3: Call an ally: Guide them 4, to a space you choose, now.)*
- *Eth — Rally* · 0 AP + 1 Charge · CD 3 · Dur 2 — Party strikes +1 for 2 turns; foes adjacent to you are Weakened 1. *(P3/E3: Party strikes +2 for 2 turns; foes adjacent to you are Weakened 1.)*

**Pattern Recognition** · Space · `hum_pattern`

- *Phys — Spot* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Reveal everything within 1 (breaks Hidden, finds hidden objectives); your next strike this turn deals +1. *(P3: Reveal everything within 4 (breaks Hidden, finds hidden objectives); your next strike this turn deals +1.)*
- *Eth — Weak Point* · 0 AP + 1 Space · CD 3 — Expose a foe: the next hit on it ignores Shield and deals +1. *(P3/E3: Expose a foe: the next hit on it ignores Shield and deals +3.)*

**Opposable Grip** · Mass · `hum_grip`

- *Phys — Manipulate* · 1 AP · CD 1 — Melee 1 and Weaken 1 (its next hit deals −1). *(P3: Melee 4 and Weaken 1 (its next hit deals −1).)*
- *Eth — Leverage* · 0 AP + 1 Mass · CD 3 — Melee 2 and Anchor the foe for 2 rounds (it cannot move). *(P3/E3: Melee 5 and Anchor the foe for 4 rounds (it cannot move).)*

**Cooperative Instinct** · Charge · `hum_coop`

- *Phys — Assist* · 1 AP · CD 1 — An ally's next strike deals +2. *(P3: An ally's next strike deals +5.)*
- *Eth — Unity* · 0 AP + 1 Charge · CD 4 — Pool your effort: an ally mints 1 temporary AP this turn. *(P3/E3: Pool your effort: an ally mints 3 temporary AP this turn.)*

**Adaptive Generalist** · Time · `hum_general`

- *Phys — Improv* · 1 AP · CD 2 — Mint 1 temporary Omni (spends as any etherion this turn). *(P3: Mint 2 temporary Omni (spends as any etherion this turn).)*
- *Eth — Mimic Form* · 0 AP + 1 Time · CD 4 — Learn on the fly: Haste: every card on your track returns 2 columns sooner, and mint 1 Omni. *(P3/E3: Learn on the fly: Haste: every card on your track returns 4 columns sooner, and mint 1 Omni.)*
